## Character Property state — active grill [editorial: stream later completed as Local Entity Property]

- selected next edge: after completed Character-grounded Interaction, the smallest
  Property slice is one confirmed `set_property` consequence inside existing
  `submit_action`, acting on the derived Character, plus one flat Character-grounded
  exact-current-Place Property read. This remains a proposed plan, not current game
  behavior.
- corrected the earlier relational proposal: immutable
  `entity_property_history` is the sole value store;
  `entity_property(entity_id, property_key_id, current_activity_id)` is only the
  current pointer. This supersedes duplicated value/type columns in the current row.
  Shared `property_key` rows retain canonical meaning/type and never centralize
  Entity values.
- proposed concrete Activity meaning: keep operation `submit_action`, add a stored
  `introduce_entity | set_property` Action-consequence discriminator, backfill
  existing Action rows as `introduce_entity` and reconstruct retries as a tagged
  accepted result rather than infer consequence from `subject` roles.
- proposed performance/concurrency direction: composite primary/foreign keys protect
  one current pointer and same-lineage predecessors; a unique canonical key
  arbitrates first use; a unique history `activity_id` makes the single v1 Property
  result reconstructable; existing User/Place locks, pointer row locking and one
  transaction prevent branches, orphan keys and partial Activity/state.
- corrected premature Trait resolution: Property work requires no Trait table. Trait
  development must retain immutable history, but its lineage, current-state and
  retirement shape remain contested and are deferred to its own later plan.
- draft-blocking choices: outward/local versus self-only Property presentation;
  Agent-created first-use canonical keys versus finite World catalog;
  acting-Character-only direct mutation versus broader ordinary-Entity targeting;
  and current structured Property precedence versus conflicting introductory
  description. The draft recommends outward/local, Agent-created exact keys,
  acting-Character-only and current Property precedence respectively.
- explicitly deferred: deletion, aliases/synonym inference, creation integration,
  arbitrary or multi-Entity targeting, Interaction consequences, reverse/global
  search and all Trait work. The formal draft is
  `dev/plans/20260813-171201-character-property-state/plan.md`; implementation
  requires explicit answers and plan acceptance.
- superseded the actor-only and creation-deferred Property draft after adversarial
  reconciliation: all four existing Entity-creation routes now propose the same
  optional 0–100 initial Properties because Character, Place and ordinary subjects
  share one Entity identity. Their existing Activity is provenance and their entire
  Entity/role/placement/Property bundle is atomic; routes without request ids retain
  their current unique/concurrency semantics.
- corrected Property-key nomenclature: a Property is deliberately compact
  `key = value`, while an explanatory sentence belongs to Trait. The proposed
  `property_key` therefore contains canonical lower-snake English key, immutable
  text/integer type and first Activity provenance—no description, aliases or synonym
  inference. Same key/type reuses; same key/different type conflicts.
- replaced actor-only change with a bounded homogeneous
  `submit_action.change_entity_property`: 1–100 unique Entity/key writes across
  1–100 exact-local Entities. Actor, current Place, co-present Characters and placed
  ordinary Entities use one role/control-agnostic eligibility rule and neutral
  unavailable result. This supports an explosion changing actor, ordinary Entity
  and another Character atomically without exposing which are User-controlled.
- restored the earlier accepted Action-or-Interaction origin rule: existing
  `submit_interaction` now proposes optional 0–100 typed Property changes whose
  subjects must be actor or explicit targets. Empty retains outward-only behavior;
  non-empty stores outward participation and Property consequence in the same
  Activity without authoring a target response. Action and Interaction remain
  separate public semantics with one private Property writer.
- resolved visibility and presentation: current Properties are outward/local facts;
  one flat exact-current-Place read covers the uniform local Entity set, and
  authorized Activity output hydrates exact typed Property changes. Current
  structured state wins over conflicting introductory prose for that key while the
  introduction remains immutable history.
- corrected persistence/cardinality: immutable history remains the sole value store
  and the current row remains pointer-only. Because one Activity may change up to
  100 Properties, history `activity_id` is indexed but not unique. Inputs are
  semantic unordered, duplicate key or Entity/key pairs reject the whole bundle,
  and stable-order key/pointer locks plus set-based history insert/current upsert
  avoid branching, N+1 work and partial results.
- narrowed the active draft to one material acceptance boundary: uniform local
  Action mutation includes other played Characters and the current Place. The plan
  recommends acceptance to avoid a control oracle and preserve causal multi-Entity
  actions, while explicitly excluding volition, response, relations, placement,
  remote subjects and dynamic/prose selectors. The plan remains draft with null
  acceptance until the User explicitly accepts this complete boundary.
- accepted the complete Local Entity Property plan on 2026-08-13: the User confirmed
  that every Entity can carry zero or more Properties, including furniture, flora,
  fauna, Characters and Places, and explicitly accepted uniform local World Action
  mutation of other Characters and the current Place. The plan is active; T1 is
  complete and T2 remains pending until the accepted `game/docs/` contract is
  deliberately published.
- clarified mutation authority: no player directly replaces their own or another
  Entity's Property and Property is never a self-owned profile/storage edit. The User
  steers and confirms, the Agent proposes an exact Action or Interaction, and World
  alone validates and writes the Activity-backed consequence. This applies uniformly
  without revealing Entity role or User control.
- bounded external factors: a later explicitly accepted deterministic mechanic may
  reuse the private Property validator/writer and Activity-backed consequence path.
  The accepted current slice supports only User-steered, confirmed Agent-authored
  Action/Interaction causes and introduces no autonomous or background Agent,
  `world_event` table, timer, scheduler or ungrounded off-screen simulation.
- published the accepted Local Entity Property contract before implementation:
  `game/docs/` and the compact MVP rule now specify one uniform 0–100 initial
  Property shape across all four Entity-creation routes, homogeneous 1–100 exact-
  local Action changes, optional 0–100 actor/explicit-target Interaction changes,
  typed authorized Activity history, one outward/local current read and the exact

  binary, schema and catalog remain explicitly the proved twelve-capability
  pre-Property state until implementation and parity evidence complete.
- completed the Local Entity Property implementation candidate and corrected its
  current authorities after review: migration, `World`, HTTP, MCP and the runtime

  current/history reads, retries, races, rollback and bounded set-based behavior

- formally closed the Local Entity Property plan after independent final re-review

## Local Entity Trait development — deterministic delivery, review active [editorial: review later completed]

- selected the next edge after delivered Property state: establish concise
  Entity-owned characterizing statements contextually and let accepted Actions or
  Interactions develop one stable Trait identity so later Agents can ground callbacks
  in current state and immutable earlier expression. This is a Proposed design
  outcome, not current behavior; the World still stores no Traits.
- retained confirmed authority and meaning: Trait is a statement such as “jumps
  unusually high,” separate from Property `key = value`, status and score. A User
  steers and confirms, an Agent authors exact input and World validates/writes the
  Activity-backed result. Statement prose never becomes executable mechanics.
- corrected the round-1 relevance wording with the User's round-2 clarification:
  every Entity owns its Property and Trait state, and whenever an Agent actually
  fetches its own Character or another eligible Entity, that Entity comes with the
  correct current associations. The Agent decides relevance; World stores no
  observer-specific Knowledge/Observation, receipt state or copy. Property and Trait
  are Entity-owned state, not Relationship domain.
- rejected every initial-creation Trait. `create_entity`, `create_character`,
  `create_entry_place` and `submit_action.introduce_entity` remain Trait-free; a
  Trait is established contextually when play makes it interesting.
- confirmed uniform exact-local Action authority over actor, current Place, ordinary
  Entity and another Character; stable World Trait id with append-only predecessor
  versions and one current pointer; establishment/development now with retirement
  deferred; and strict non-executable statement prose.
- corrected origin and package scope: both Action and Interaction may establish or
  develop Traits through one private writer. Interaction Trait subjects are exactly
  actor plus explicit targets; its optional `trait_change[0..100]` may mix lifecycle
  items and coexist atomically with Property changes. Action uses one closed mixed
  `trait_change[1..100]`. Interaction Trait change is an explicit World consequence
  and never a target-authored response, thought, consent or volition.
- resolved conflict and cause scope: exact normalized active duplicates and unchanged
  development reject; semantic contradictions remain valid; a current lineage
  supersedes only itself and gains no automatic precedence over another Trait,
  Property or description. Only explicit confirmed Action/Interaction are executable
  causes; no future external deterministic writer is present now.
- resolved round 3: `list_entity_at_current_place` stays compact;
  `get_character` and new exact-local `get_entity_at_current_place` return one
  combined typed current Property/Trait association page with one opaque cursor,
  default 25 and maximum 100. The cursor binds selected Entity and nullable current
  Place revision so continuation pages cannot mix snapshots. Activity, creation and
  mutation Entity/Place values remain compact references, not recursive fetches.
  The new scoped read replaces `list_entity_property_at_current_place`, keeping the
  player catalog at exactly thirteen.
- resolved authorship/confirmation: the Agent authors the Trait consequence and
  naturally previews every exact Entity/lifecycle item, Trait id plus current/new
  statement where applicable and outward prose. The User accepts or rejects the
  whole package, receives no direct Trait editor and never gets an undisclosed
  post-confirmation Trait mutation.
- resolved technical storage vocabulary and content bound: PostgreSQL uses `text`,
  not `LONGTEXT`; every immutable statement is trimmed, non-NUL and 1–4,000 Unicode
  characters. Richer causality belongs in Activity prose.
- resolved exact duplicate scope: one mixed package rejects duplicate establish
  `(entity_id, normalized statement)`, duplicate develop `trait_id`, exact-current
  development and establishment of an exact statement already active on the Entity;
  semantic near-duplicates and contradictions remain accepted.
- proposed a scalable but unaccepted three-table persistence seam:
  `entity_trait` owns immutable stable identity and Entity, `entity_trait_version`
  stores append-only Activity-backed statements and predecessor links, and
  `entity_trait_current` stores only the current version pointer. No creation route
  touches it. Existing `submit_action` and `submit_interaction` deepen; no new
  mutation tool or observer relation is proposed.
- corrected the reviewed persistence/freshness seam: `entity_trait` carries no
  duplicate establishing Activity pointer. The unique null-predecessor
  `entity_trait_version` root is the sole establishing Activity provenance.
  Development input remains only stable Trait id plus new statement; the request's
  expected Place revision and locked current pointer select the authoritative
  predecessor atomically, so no contradictory predecessor input is added.

## First complete investigation and discovery loop — grill active

### Confirmed direction

- selected / reframed: the User selected the first complete investigation-and-
  discovery loop as the next planning edge and requested a fully Terry-aligned
  [draft plan](../../../../dev/plans/20260814-204007-first-investigation-discovery-loop/plan.md).
  The former separate queued roll and commit are one outcome: ground at the exact
  current Place, let World resolve before Agent authorship, and end in a meaningful
  zero or exactly one shared result with provenance and Activity. Neither a roll-only
  plumbing milestone nor an ordinary mutation relabelled as discovery is sufficient.
- planning boundary: the combined backlog item is restored to `Now / Proposed`
  after the post-correction Property/Trait validation closed. Discovery selection
  authorizes grill and plan maintenance only; `game/docs/`,
  schema, runtime and the thirteen-capability executable surface remain unchanged
  until every material decision is resolved and the complete plan is explicitly
  accepted.
- resolved / corrected authority question: review of the earlier discovery choices
  establishes that discovery is not `create_entity` or
  `submit_action.introduce_entity` under another name. The rejected
  `introduce_entity`-style roll envelope remains rejected. Discovery uniquely owns
  World admission and chance resolution before Agent authorship plus attempt/result
  provenance; a later accepted concrete result may reuse Entity state without making
  the roll return a persistence helper.
- reaffirmed direction: the User requires a powerful, understandable discovery
  mechanism with a roll. The Agent requests an investigation; World performs exactly
  one authoritative roll only after admitting its established context. The caller
  gets no separate roll button, seed, odds, result-count or retry-count control. A
  retry remains the same roll, and an ineligible request rolls nothing.
- corrected after cross-conversation audit: the subsequent one-roll-per-unchanged-
  context recommendation was wrong and is rejected. The original discussion in the
  `Aicadia — Character en Place-fundament` task confirms the User's accepted Q45/Q46
  choices already preserved earlier in this file: several attempt-specific,
  scope-bound `roll_token`s may coexist across conversations, and every new explicit
  Agent request that World admits is a fresh independent attempt even without an
  intervening World change. One Character-wide operational policy applies before
  rolling, so conversation count grants no extra throughput; an equal idempotency key
  is the same attempt, and only overlapping accepted changes stale existing tokens.
  A zero therefore ends one attempt but never permanently locks unchanged World-state.
- confirmed architecture constraint: the User requires the complete discovery build
  to follow Terry with clear ownership and no monolithic implementation files. The
  draft plan now fixes one deep private investigation Module behind the existing
  public `World` Interface; chance policy, Character-wide attempt/retry state,
  volatile result authority and atomic concrete commit have distinct owners, while
  concrete Entity/Property/Activity invariants remain in their current World owners.
  HTTP and MCP stay thin transport Adapters. `game/src/world/mod.rs` and the current
  all-in-one `game/src/server.rs` are not landing places for new discovery behavior; the
  accepted build must first give each touched concern one reason to change and must
  not create pass-through modules, a second public game seam or speculative ports.

### Open decisions

- Q1 / current frontier: which one concrete positive result proves the first complete
  discovery loop? The draft recommends one newly discovered Entity at the exact
  current Place with bounded initial Properties and no Traits, while preserving the
  already-confirmed distinction from ordinary Entity introduction.
- blocked behind Q1: eligible chance-table entries, weights and meaningful zero/
  history; structural duplicate/conflict behavior; the concrete Character-wide
  operational admission mechanism and threshold; retry-stable parallel token/secret
  lifecycle; atomic provenance and Activity; and public operation/error/Agent
  vocabulary. The evidence boundary is already fixed to deterministic World/HTTP/
  MCP, restart/concurrency, fake-Agent and two-Character shared-state proof; any paid
  live-model validation requires a separate explicit plan and authorization.

  thirteen-tool catalog together. It authorizes no paid model validation.
- delivered: migration `0009_uniform_entity_state.sql`, World, HTTP, MCP and the

## First complete investigation and discovery loop — grill resumed

- accepted build rule: on 2026-08-15 the User required that every developed
  component be designed and built for millions of concurrent players. `AGENTS.md`
  gains the compact heuristic `Built For Massive Concurrency`: locks, transactions,
  revisions, queries and admission scope to the smallest protected subject, reads
  stay bounded, and process-local correctness state, server affinity, global
  locks/revisions/counters and hot shared rows are rejected. KISS is sharpened
  rather than weakened: predicted scale never justifies prebuilt infrastructure, but
  no design may preclude scale. The current single entry Place without movement is
  a known contention risk owned by the later movement edge, not by the discovery
  slice.
- consequence for the discovery draft: the investigation start takes only the
  per-User lock and no Place lock; a positive result is not bound to the exact-Place
  revision; the commit takes the Place lock like every Action; admission counts
  durable per-User attempt state under the User lock; positive meta-data stays
  bounded and typed. These are draft directions pending the remaining grill answers.
- decided / Q1 resolved: on 2026-08-15 the User accepted the discovery rule and the
  first concrete positive result. Rule: what already existed in the World without
  anyone making it—plants, tracks, ore, springs, ruin fragments and later new
  Places—enters only through an admitted investigation and roll; what a Character
  makes, brings or places remains ordinary confirmed Action introduction. World
  cannot enforce the distinction; the Agent contract states it and deterministic
  Agent evidence exercises it. First result: exactly one found thing at the exact
  current Place, stored as an ordinary placed Entity with Agent-authored Properties
  and Traits; World types no kind and supplies no semantic direction. Discovering a
  new Place is named as the second result kind and belongs to the later movement
  edge. This supersedes the earlier no-Trait recommendation for Q1.
- decided / positive payload: a positive result carries bounded typed meta-data—the
  safe current-Place view, the Place's own current Property/Trait state, a fixed
  bounded list of the most recent accepted discoveries at that Place and generic
  limits (`result_count: 1`, kind: Entity at current Place)—plus opaque attempt
  authority. No counts, ranking, kind list or server prose; deeper context comes
  through existing paged reads. The rich broad-inspection payload from the earlier
  exploration remains outside this slice.
- decided / confirmation boundary: the Agent may start an investigation without
  User confirmation—it is admission plus a World roll, not a player-visible World
  change—while the authored find is previewed completely and naturally and committed
  only after the User's explicit confirmation, exactly like every existing Action.
  The User still holds no authority over roll, odds, result or whether something was
  found; confirmation covers only whether this authored package enters the World.
  Investigating remains the Agent's own intelligent choice within the conversation.
- decided / zero and Place pointer: a zero result writes no Activity and never moves
  the exact-Place pointer; only the internal attempt row exists for retry identity
  and admission. Likewise an admitted but not yet committed positive result writes
  no Activity. The Agent renders zero in-world as one honest unsuccessful search and
  continues with ordinary play or a later investigation; operational admission
  bounds repetition. The backlog wording "truthful next situation" is narrowed to
  "zero honestly ends one attempt". Shared or personal zero Activity is rejected
  because same-Place revision churn would break co-present Actions at scale.
- decided / Q5 representation, superseding the signed `roll_token` direction: one
  durable `investigation_attempt` relation—id, requested_by_user_id, request_id,
  request fingerprint, Character, exact Place, outcome and the consuming Activity—
  is the attempt's only home, and the unguessable attempt id bound to the User
  context is the opaque commit authority. The commit locks the User, requires an
  own, positive, unconsumed attempt and consumes it in the same transaction as the
  accepted result. No secret, HMAC, key rotation, launcher configuration or new
  cryptography crate is introduced; restart and multi-instance safety follow from
  PostgreSQL alone. The earlier client-carried authenticated token is superseded
  because retry identity, Character-wide admission and one-time consumption already
  require durable per-attempt state, making the token a second truth without gain.
  Optimistic scope-bound parallelism (Q45/Q46) is preserved: several unconsumed
  positive attempts may coexist and unrelated changes never stale them.
- decided / chance source and test seam: production randomness comes from OS entropy
  through the standard `rand` crate behind one private World-owned chance source;
  tests inject a scripted zero/positive sequence at World construction. This is the
  one earned internal seam (two real adapters); it is reachable only through World
  construction, never through HTTP, MCP, an environment variable or an operator
  switch. Two-Character and observer evidence therefore runs as in-process
  PostgreSQL-backed Rust tests; the token-free fake Agent suite needs no real roll.
  PostgreSQL `random()` was rejected for unreliable seeding across pooled
  connections and a keyed deterministic draw for reintroducing secret management.
- decided / Q2 chance policy: chance resolution is its own component. A private
  `ChancePolicy` owns one pure deterministic probability function and its typed
  parameters; attempt orchestration gathers the typed signals through bounded
  existing Activity reads; the injected chance source supplies the draw. Slice 1
  uses one signal—recent discovery density at the exact Place, the number of
  accepted discoveries among the last W Activities at that Place—so the read is
  bounded by definition and the World's ordinary activity lets a well-searched Place
  recover without a clock, counter column or global aggregate. Formula
  `p = p_min + (p_max - p_min) * 2^(-n/h)` with defaults p_max 1/2, p_min 1/10,
  h 6, W 48; the earlier hyperbolic proposal with a hard count cap was rejected as an
  arbitrary cutoff. Parameters live once in the chance component with documented
  defaults in `game/docs/`; changing them is a documented contract change and no
  runtime configuration exists until an operator scenario earns it. Own misses,
  elapsed Character time and World-wide totals stay outside the formula: the
  no-pity choice stands and global aggregates are unbounded. The Agent sees
  saturation qualitatively through the recent discoveries in the positive payload;
  the draw remains hidden.
- decided / Q4 admission: before any roll World locks the User and applies two
  bounded per-User rules over the durable attempt rows. Throughput: at most A
  admitted attempts per rolling hour (default 12); an equal-idempotency retry is the
  existing attempt and never counts again; rejection creates no attempt, rolls
  nothing and returns one neutral error. Hoarding: at most P unconsumed positive
  attempts (default 3); admitting a further attempt beyond that voids the oldest
  unconsumed positive, so a lost attempt id can never deadlock a Character and no
  listable stock of pending finds exists. Both thresholds are typed parameters with
  documented defaults, hidden from play and never inputs to odds; they aggregate
  across every conversation of the Character. Reject-until-consumed with expiry and
  rate-only were rejected (second clock, or an unbounded stock).
- decided / Q3 candidate conflicts and freshness: a discovery commit rejects only
  invalid values (the same name/description/Property/Trait rules as Entity
  introduction), an attempt that is not this User's, not positive, already consumed
  or voided (one neutral error without distinction), a Character no longer at the
  attempt's Place (checked although movement does not yet exist) and a reused
  request id with different normalized content. Semantically similar finds remain
  valid; there is no name uniqueness and no exact-Place revision binding. The
  attempt's bounded dependency set in slice 1 is therefore its own state plus the
  Character's placement, and only later result kinds add explicit dependency ids.
- decided / Q6 history and atomic completion: an accepted find is one new Activity
  operation with the acting Character as actor, the exact Place as context, the new
  Entity as `subject`, the Place as `location`, canonical Agent-authored prose with
  Action prose bounds included in the request fingerprint, and the attempt's
  `consumed_by_activity_id` as the sole attempt/result provenance link—no new
  `activity` column, generic discovery table or event payload. The same transaction
  creates and places the Entity with its initial Properties and Traits, consumes the
  attempt, inserts the Activity and roles and advances the exact-Place pointer under
  the Place lock; any failure rolls back everything. Zero and unconsumed positive
  attempts leave no Activity.
- decided / Q7 public operations: `start_investigation` (`POST /api/investigation`,
  200, input `{request_id}`, tagged output `zero` or `positive` with attempt id,
  generic limits and bounded context) and `submit_discovery` (`POST /api/discovery`,
  201, input `{request_id, attempt_id, prose, find{name, description, property,
  trait}}`, output activity/entity/place analogous to an accepted Action). Neutral
  canonical errors: `investigation_not_admitted` (429, no attempt, no roll, hidden
  threshold), `investigation_request_conflict` and `discovery_request_conflict`
  (409), `discovery_attempt_unavailable` (409, no distinction between foreign,
  non-positive, consumed, voided or other-Place attempts) and `invalid_discovery`
  (400) beside existing Character, value and Property-key errors.
  `Activity.operation` gains `submit_discovery`; the catalog becomes fifteen player
  capabilities with full World/HTTP/MCP parity. Zero/positive remain the canonical
  result words; a bare `investigate` verb was rejected under the operation-name rule.
- process decision: the behavior-preserving server split stays the first step of
  the plan's protocol task rather than a separate plan, and a verification-only
  baseline task precedes implementation: the User commits the pending uniform
  Entity-state build and the complete ladder is green on that commit before any
  discovery change. The draft plan is rewritten with every resolved answer, the
  accepted operation names, migration `0010`, the attempt relation, the chance and
  admission components and the fifteen-capability catalog; `dev/docs/concept/discovery.md`
  and the backlog item are aligned. The plan remains `draft` and unaccepted; the
  item remains `Next / Proposed` behind the active Sol-medium validation.
- accepted execution boundary: on 2026-08-15 the User explicitly asked to carry the
  complete discovery plan through under Root orchestration with Sol Medium and Sol
  High sub-agents where dependency-safe. The plan is `active`, discovery is the sole
  `Now / Active` edge and T0 verifies the required committed green uniform-state
  baseline before any contract or runtime implementation. The dependency graph
  remains sequential where tasks share authoritative or runtime surfaces; parallel
  read-only verification does not relax that gate. No paid model call is authorized.
- refined implementation order and test seam: T0 review exposed two contradictions
  without changing the accepted outcome. Migration `0010` must extend the existing
  Trait-version Activity validator for `submit_discovery`, or a valid find with
  initial Traits would fail. Start must rate-admit, draw and insert first, then only
  when that inserted attempt is positive and exceeds the hoarding cap void the
  oldest live positive with a valid FK to the new row; pre-roll voiding was rejected
  because zero must not discard a positive and the referenced attempt would not yet
  exist. Scripted chance remains behind a crate-private test-only construction
  helper with chance-dependent tests inside the crate, preserving the single public
  `World::new` seam.
- reopened T1 contract frontier: independent Sol Medium and Sol High review stopped
  contract publication before edits because the accepted positive payload combined
  mutable, cumulatively unbounded Place state with byte-identical retries while the
  attempt stored neither snapshot nor revision. The plan returns to `draft` and the
  item to `Now / Proposed`. The recommended correction returns only stored outcome,
  attempt authority and immutable limits, then requires existing current reads;
  drops the redundant start fingerprint and unreachable start-conflict error; pins
  submit output to Activity, Entity and Place without duplicating current state;
  orders retry, attempt availability and semantic validation; treats unplaced or
  moved attempts neutrally; uses database time plus `(created_at, id)` FIFO; keeps
  attempt and Activity request-id namespaces distinct; and publishes final contract
  prose without delivery status. A partial Place/Activity ordering index is required
  to make the last-48 chance window bounded at a hot Place. These recommendations
  await explicit User reacceptance; no `game/docs/` or runtime implementation began.
- reaccepted T1 contract correction: on 2026-08-15 the User accepted every
  recommendation in the complete reopened frontier: stable start output followed by
  current reads; exact Activity/Entity/Place submit output; conventional normalized
  input and retry/error precedence; neutral unplaced/moved attempt handling;
  database-authoritative rolling time and deterministic FIFO; separate attempt and
  Activity request-id namespaces; and final `game/docs` contract publication without
  delivery-status prose. The plan returns to `active` and T1 resumes; the bounded
  Place-Activity index is the accepted technical consequence, not a new mechanic.
- method / discovery evidence boundary: a self-authored static trace was rejected as
  clean-room Agent evidence even though its validator passed. Runtime/PostgreSQL,
  adapter, executed fake-Agent orchestration and static validator mutations are four
  non-substitutable claim classes. The corrected token-free runner executes the
  isolated JSONL controller phases and derives its trace only after their event,
  identity, confirmation, state/history and recovery gates pass; static mutations
  prove only validator rejection.
- completed / first investigation and discovery loop: on 2026-08-15 World, HTTP,
  MCP, the Agent contract and the fifteen-capability catalog delivered the accepted
  zero/positive investigation and confirmed found-Entity result with durable
  provenance. Deterministic, real-restart and token-free controller evidence passed;
  no paid-model, repeated-natural-roll, movement or fun-quality claim was made.
  Delivery history and current status: see [discovery
  evidence](../../evidence/discovery.md).

## Discovery loop review corrections — accepted

- reviewed / accepted: on 2026-08-15 an independent three-reader review of the
  delivered discovery loop (ladder rerun green: format, Clippy, 147/147 PostgreSQL
  tests, three shell suites) found no P0, one P1 and bounded P2/P3 debt. The User
  accepted the [correction plan](../../../../dev/plans/20260815-180754-discovery-review-corrections/plan.md)
  after committing the delivery as `60ce27c`. Accepted with it: the discovery
  fingerprint reuses the existing helpers and tags each list (its earlier shape let
  Property and Trait content collide into a false equal retry) without a
  compatibility path because no shipped rows exist; the investigation result field
  `limits` becomes singular `limit`; HTTP status mapping and the wire code list
  become compiler-exhaustive; `game/docs/domain.md` is the one home of chance and
  admission parameters; `instruction/15-recovery.md` owns recovery for the three
  discovery errors. No zero/positive, threshold, formula, payload or name change
  beyond `limit`. Delegated execution: one sub-agent per task under Root review.
- completed / review corrections: on 2026-08-15 T1–T3 ran as three delegated
  sub-agent tasks under Root review and T4 closed the plan. Corrected: the discovery
  fingerprint (shared helpers, tag plus item count per list—Root's review of the
  first tag-only revision found a second collision through tag-lookalike keys and
  statements, now also proved to conflict); resolution moved into the chance
  component and dead branches removed; production SQL constants proved by in-crate
  plan and window tests (a microsecond-exact inclusivity is unreachable through
  `statement_timestamp()` and is protected by the shared constant); `limit`
  singular; wildcard-free exhaustive code and status maps; both-adapter proof of
  every discovery rejection; composition-only server root; template-conformant
  start description; single recovery owner; single parameter home in
  `game/docs/domain.md`; corrected evidence pointers, concept banner and old plan
  row. Ladder green at 152/152 Rust tests plus all shell suites; the Trait
  candidate digest was refrozen for the changed sources. No paid model call.
  Delivery history and current status: see [discovery evidence](../../evidence/discovery.md#review-corrections-2026-08-15).

## Mass concurrency and living World exploration — open

- researched / scale boundary: on 2026-08-16 a PostgreSQL primary-source review,
  current-code concurrency audit and living-World design exploration established
  the central split: independent reads, Activity appends and state subjects may be
  scaled separately, while incompatible writes to one canonical current fact must
  still be ordered or explicitly rejected. Current contextual writers instead share
  one Place row and several paged queries are output-bounded without yet being
  proven work-bounded at adversarial occupancy or history. No current million-User
  or production-load claim exists. See [the sourced research](../../research/massively-concurrent-dynamic-world.md)
  and [active concept direction](../concurrency-and-world-dynamics.md).
- user direction / open grill: the User rejected a whole Place as the intended
  final conflict unit because it would constrain discovery and enrichment, and
  required the design to remain genuinely playable for millions of simultaneous
  Users in one persistent World, including hot Entity and hot Place scenarios.
  Scale must serve the best game behavior rather than preserve an easy lock or
  partition. Exact dependency, admission, discovery, movement, time, safety and
  production-proof choices remain open and will be resolved through a design-tree
  grill before any consequential build plan is accepted.

## Multiplayer observation and realtime World change — open

- user direction / research focus: the User required the next substantial plan to
  concentrate deeply on multiplayer, concurrency and high World dynamism. Many
  Characters may share one Place and active hosts should be able to notice what is
  changing there; the concrete test is one large stone falling and becoming
  grounded context for eligible Characters, potentially through sight or hearing.
  Research must precede the continued grill and the grill remains one question per
  turn by explicit User instruction.
- researched / four-plane boundary: first-party MMO, engine, distributed-system and
  MCP evidence separates one authoritative consequence and Activity, event-time
  observation eligibility, transient realtime delivery and actual LLM consumption.
  One occurrence can be stored once and fanned out through coalescible wake-ups with
  authoritative refetch; one million active recipients still imply roughly one
  million network deliveries, but not one million World rows or Agent runs. See
  [multiplayer research](../../research/multiplayer-concurrency-and-world-observation.md).
- identified / recovery and ordering risk: current newest-first `(occurred_at, id)`
  history is not by itself a no-gap forward commit cursor once independent local
  transactions can commit out of order. Any later delivery watermark remains
  transport or projection state rather than Place/World revision. Whether every
  ambient occurrence must be recovered is an open game decision, not an
  infrastructure assumption.
- open / first decision: offline fictional presence versus explicit active
  attention determines whether later reads may represent direct perception, only a
  public Place chronicle or neither. No multiplayer build plan, sensory mechanic,
  notification capability or contract change is accepted yet.
- accepted development workflow: on 2026-08-16 the User accepted the
  [multiplayer-lab plan](../../../../dev/plans/20260816-153410-multiplayer-lab/plan.md).
  `lab/` is the lasting general experimental workbench and
  `dev/lab/multiplayer/` is its first current track. Lab code may remain deliberately
  rough and retained with a verdict and status, but it never becomes production
  behavior or a runtime dependency merely by surviving. The multiplayer grill asks
  exactly one material question per User turn and earns a small in-memory,
  Postgres, MCP or explicit bounded Agent experiment only when evidence can change
  the pending decision; no background Agent or token spend is introduced. The User
  further required every grill question to include Root's argued preference through
  Terry's player-value, contract, domain, smallest-safe-slice and exact-evidence
  gates, and authorized bounded subagents across the plan for independent research,
  adversarial critique and experiments. Root remains responsible for integration
  and the final recommendation.
- decided direction / multiplayer Q1: a Character still held by its player remains
  canonically placed across User or host disconnects, but presence without active
  attention creates no personal claim of having seen or heard ambient occurrences.
  Reconnection may expose current state, public traces and applicable personal
  consequences through later accepted bounded reads. Presence alone grants neither
  blanket permission nor blanket immunity for other mechanics. “Player-controlled”
  and “active attention” remain separate working concepts, not current status fields.
  When a player later takes another Character and abandons the old one, the old
  Character retains its World identity and may continue through a future NPC
  lifecycle rather than disappear. Exact abandonment, control, NPC reasoning,
  cadence and safety behavior remain deferred; no server LLM, background Agent or
  autonomous simulation is authorized by this direction. The next grill decision is
  the explicit boundary that starts and ends active attention.
- decided direction / multiplayer Q2: active attention for a player-controlled
  Character begins only after its authenticated host obtains an authoritative
  current-Character and Place baseline and explicitly subscribes there. It ends on
  unsubscribe, connection loss, Character switch or Place departure. While active,
  coalescible hints may cause bounded authoritative refetch and the host may buffer
  relevant context for the next explicit User-owned Agent turn. There is no durable
  World attention flag, per-Activity personal copy or automatic LLM call. An
  always-connected host may keep listening like an online MMO client; transport
  availability remains distinct from World truth and Agent knowledge.
- accepted documentation boundary: every multiplayer grill choice is written to the
  active concept and period log before the next question, so repository authorities
  rather than conversation memory carry the design. Sourced factual findings plus
  citations belong in `dev/docs/research/`; later accepted current behavior belongs in
  `game/docs/`. The lab is deliberately separate and owns only experimental
  artifacts, observations and bounded verdicts that link to the choice they test.
- accepted method direction / build pending: the User required the repository-memory
  and experiment-separation methodology to govern future Aicadia work through
  `AGENTS.md` and the relevant build, grilling and prototype skills. A read-only
  audit found three concrete conflicts: whole-frontier grilling ignored the explicit
  one-question cadence, prototype capture required disposal outside main and direct
  folding into real code, and build-aicadia did not yet require rereading the
  repository trail before the next question. The multiplayer-lab plan returned to
  `draft` with one bounded methodology task; no global rule or skill is changed until
  that expanded plan is explicitly reaccepted.
- completed method build: after explicit reacceptance on 2026-08-16, `AGENTS.md` now
  requires repository-backed resumption, per-answer concept/log updates before the
  next material grill question and strict separation between exploration, decision
  history, sourced research, current game contract and retained lab evidence.
  Build-aicadia executes that sequence and fixes the period-log destination;
  grilling preserves its default design-tree frontier while honoring an explicit
  one-question round and authorization-bounded delegation; prototype treats
  “throwaway” as an experimental quality and promotion boundary, permits a
  project-designated retained lab and requires production behavior to be redesigned
  separately. All three skills passed the official syntax validator. Independent
  dry-run subagents then recovered Q2 from the repository, produced exactly one
  Terry-recommended Q3 and placed a hypothetical retained prototype and verdict in
  the correct lab track without promoting it. No game contract or runtime behavior
  changed.
- decided direction / multiplayer Q3: a Character that arrives later at the exact
  Place may retrieve a bounded public occurrence such as the stone fall through
  local Place history, but may never present that learned history as something it
  personally saw or heard. Current state and physical traces remain separate from
  the one stored Activity; no personal observation receipt or recipient copy is
  created. Private or explicitly targeted Interactions remain excluded from
  unrelated Characters under the current local Activity contract. A future mechanic
  must make public occurrence meaning explicit rather than derive it from prose,
  arbitrary Properties or Traits. Aftermath-only discovery was rejected because it
  erases other players' durable contribution; personal-memory treatment was rejected
  as false omniscience. This is concept direction, not yet a `game/docs/` or runtime
  change. The next grill branch is generic local knowability versus explicit typed
  live sensory channels.
- challenged and corrected recommendation / multiplayer Q4 open: the User argued
  that per-Activity `visible` and `audible` metadata is overkill when occurrence and
  Character locations can be compared and the Agent can interpret the result. Two
  independent critiques agreed that typed channels are not earned for the first
  slice, but found that location alone cannot make the Agent authoritative for
  publicness, privacy, silence, concealment, range, occlusion or audience. The
  revised Terry recommendation is a smaller hybrid: a concrete operation contract
  marks an occurrence `public local`; World derives active attention and exact Place
  equality and filters before delivery; the authorized Agent chooses only natural
  sensory presentation, which creates no mechanics or durable perception. Private
  or targeted Interaction remains hidden from unrelated bystanders. Hidden Actions,
  whispers, adjacent-Place propagation, occlusion and sensory capabilities remain
  unsupported until one concrete mechanic earns a bounded deterministic rule. This
  challenge corrects the recommendation but makes no accepted Q4 choice or current
  game-contract change yet.
- decided direction / multiplayer Q4: the User accepted the Terry-corrected hybrid
  with explicit ownerships. A concrete operation contract owns whether an accepted
  occurrence is public and local; World derives Character, active attention and
  exact Place and authorizes before returning content; Activity stores the
  occurrence and local history once; host transport owns only subscription,
  coalescible hints and bounded temporary context; Agent chooses natural sensory
  presentation after authorization and creates no mechanic or durable knowledge;
  User owns every Agent invocation and token spend; and each future hidden-audience,
  whisper, propagation, occlusion or sensory-capability exception belongs to the
  concrete mechanic that earns it. Typed `visible`/`audible` channels in the first
  slice and pure location-only Agent authorization are both rejected. `Public local`
  remains a working policy label rather than a canonical type, field or table, so
  `dev/CONTEXT.md` does not change. This remains concept direction, not current game or
  runtime behavior.
- experiment evidence / multiplayer Q1–Q4 ownership: the retained
  `dev/lab/multiplayer/01-observation-ownership` fixture passed all eight bounded
  scenarios and kept one shared World Activity list while subscription, opaque hint
  and bounded context remained host state and Agent knowledge changed only on an
  explicit User-turn command. It also kept a private same-Place Interaction from an
  unrelated subscribed bystander. Verdict `supported`, artifact `kept`. This proves
  only that the accepted ownership split is internally representable in the fixed
  in-memory model. The host, hints, refetch and Agent were simulated: no real MCP,
  LLM, Postgres, ordering, network, concurrency or scale claim was tested. A later
  bounded real-Agent smoke may test interpretation after World authorization but
  cannot establish authorization or privacy correctness.
- decided method / lab and build evidence layers: after asking whether the first
  lab used real Agents, the User required the distinction to govern both lab and
  build methodology. `AGENTS.md`, `build-aicadia`, the reusable `prototype` skill
  and `lab/README.md` now require every experiment to name its real and simulated
  database, protocol, host, Agent and client seams. Deterministic evidence is limited
  to the exact World implementation and fixture it executes; a simulated World never
  proves production authorization or privacy. A direct MCP or Agent smoke proves
  only the exercised integration and interpretation and cannot borrow delivery,
  correctness or scale claims. An independent subagent initially found and caused
  correction of that simulated-World ambiguity, then passed the revised wording;
  both changed skills pass the official validator. No game or runtime behavior
  changed.
- corrected and decided method / Rust technical labs: the User challenged apparent
  Python testing because Aicadia is a Rust application. Python had only executed the
  official `SKILL.md` validator; the first observation state model was actually
  HTML/JavaScript. The User nevertheless chose the stronger rule that technical
  state, concurrency, ordering, database and protocol labs default to small
  standalone Rust crates. HTML/JavaScript is reserved for experiments whose evidence
  is human visual or semantic interaction. Keep one canonical state machine and
  migrate the existing observation fixture rather than maintaining two versions.
  Production may use lab invariants, scenarios and measurements, but experimental
  code is never copied or promoted. `lab/README.md`, `build-aicadia`, `prototype`
  and its interactive-logic procedure now encode that separation; both changed
  skills pass the official validator. This changes experimental methodology only,
  not current game or runtime behavior.
- feasibility evidence / real Agent and MCP multiplayer seam: a read-only audit of
  the current Rust server, protocol, deferrals and existing playtest machinery found
  that Aicadia MCP is stateless and has no subscribe/listen or notification
  capability. Therefore a current “live subscription” Agent test would simulate
  attention, hint and host buffering even if Postgres, World, HTTP, MCP and the model
  were real. One later bounded call can honestly test public Place history versus
  personal-memory wording and private Interaction filtering; a three-call
  live/history/private presentation smoke should wait until the catch-up contract is
  chosen. The existing owned disposable-database and clean-room Agent machinery can
  provide isolation, authoritative readback and cleanup, but its fixed four-call
  Property scenario cannot be relabeled as multiplayer evidence. No call, token
  spend, database write or runtime change occurred in this audit.
- completed experiment migration / observation ownership: the one canonical
  `dev/lab/multiplayer/01-observation-ownership` state model now lives in a standalone
  dependency-free Rust crate and the superseded HTML/JavaScript implementation was
  removed after all eight scenarios passed in Rust. The experiment still supports
  only its fixed in-memory ownership model; database, MCP, Agent, delivery, ordering
  and scale seams remain simulated or absent. This records the User-selected
  technical-lab medium and does not change current game behavior.
- current implementation evidence / Place-wide mutation lane: a full local
  Rust/Postgres baseline completed 152 passing tests with one deliberately ignored
  server test. Existing concurrency cases proved that contextual writers both
  compare the same Place revision and wait behind the locked Place row. This makes
  the present correctness boundary executable, but is not a throughput, hosted
  deployment or million-User result. Removing only `expected_place_revision` would
  not remove the lane while every accepted writer still advances
  `place.latest_activity_id`.
- experiment evidence / subject-scoped conflict semantics: the retained
  `dev/lab/multiplayer/02-subject-conflict` Rust fixture passed ten fixed interleavings
  without a Place revision. Independent same-Place subjects and different Property
  keys could both succeed; one current or expected-absent Property had one winner;
  moved-actor, accepted replay, changed fingerprint and rejected-request correction
  retained exact Activity counts. Rejected submissions deliberately reserve no
  accepted request identity. Verdict `supported` for the in-memory model and status
  `kept`; PostgreSQL locks, race safety, persistence, crash recovery and the product
  contract remain unproved.
- identified T3 ownership split / decision open: four responsibilities must not be
  collapsed. A concrete operation owns the bounded typed dependency shape; the
  Agent may return expected typed values, absence or opaque versions it actually
  read; World always revalidates ownership, placement, locality, eligibility and
  bounds; and PostgreSQL locking plus accepted request identity make the checked
  mutation and its one Activity atomic and replayable. Expected absence needs a
  coordinator because no Property row exists yet; conservatively locking affected
  Entity rows in stable order is a candidate, not a schema decision. The next grill
  asks whether freshness is operation-scoped: all bounded causal reads, writes and
  mandatory World invariants conflict when stale, while unrelated Place change does
  not. Whole-Place freshness and unsafe write-set-only checking are not accepted.
- experiment evidence / real PostgreSQL subject conflicts: the retained
  `dev/lab/multiplayer/03-postgres-subject-conflict` scratch-schema fixture formally
  recorded verdict `refuted` for the combined strong hypothesis. Existing Door
  Property work bypassed a held Place lock, a hot Stone Entity lock did not block the
  Door, and same-current plus expected-absence races each yielded one accepted
  history path and one dependency conflict. Retry/fingerprint and forced-failure
  rollback stayed atomic. However, Entity introduction through an honest placement
  foreign key requested `KEY SHARE` on Place and hit bounded SQLSTATE `55P03` behind
  an old `FOR UPDATE` Place writer. Two normal introductions without that exclusive
  writer both accepted because their integrity locks were compatible. Five SQLx
  tests and a separate zero-leak cleanup audit passed. This rejects total Place-row
  independence, supports the narrower subject-isolation candidate only in the
  scratch fixture and adds a production migration requirement: old exclusive Place
  writers cannot coexist with a claim of independent introduction. No current game,
  schema or runtime choice changed.
- decided direction / multiplayer Q5 operation-scoped freshness: the User accepted
  that a prepared Action becomes stale only when a bounded typed fact on which its
  concrete operation causally depended has changed, or when a mandatory World
  invariant would otherwise fail. Thus a Stone roll may ignore an unrelated wall
  change at the same Place but must conflict if the Door it read as open has closed.
  The concrete operation owns the allowed dependency shape; the Agent returns
  expected typed facts it actually read; World independently revalidates ownership,
  placement, locality, eligibility and bounds. Whole-Place freshness was rejected
  as false contention and write-set-only checking as causally unsafe. This chooses
  product conflict semantics, not dependency tokens, schema, lock granularity or a
  public contract.
- directed experiment refinement / compare credible PostgreSQL coordinators: after
  asking whether experiment 03 had tried multiple configurations, the User clarified
  that the retained lab may compare several setups to find the best small form. The
  earlier evidence used many interleavings but one primary Entity-lock strategy and
  one 150 ms lock bound, so it was not a strategy comparison. A bounded follow-up
  may compare whole-Place, affected-Entity and exact-Property coordination plus only
  settings that can alter correctness, isolation or bounded contention. It remains
  experimental and cannot select production architecture through a microbenchmark.
- decided lab method / optional comparative variants: the User generalized that any
  retained lab experiment may try several credible setups or settings when the
  situation calls for it, but no experiment is required to manufacture variants.
  `lab/README.md` now makes comparison decision-driven: alternatives share one
  fixture and criteria, while parameter sweeps, local benchmark rankings and variant
  counts cannot stand in for a product or production-scale conclusion.
- experiment evidence / comparative PostgreSQL conflict strategies: the retained
  `dev/lab/multiplayer/04-postgres-conflict-strategies` lab held Q5 dependency semantics
  constant while comparing Place, Entity, hybrid current-row/Entity-fallback and
  exact Property-slot coordination under `READ COMMITTED`, plus one controlled
  `SERIALIZABLE` write-skew challenger. Ten focused SQLx tests and a separate
  zero-leak cleanup audit passed. Basic same-current, same-absence and one explicit
  bidirectional Stone-clear/Door-open invariant converged under all four explicit
  coordinators. The initial hybrid preference was then refuted: independent requests
  mixing opposite `Absent`/`Current` dependencies formed a lock-class/FK cycle and
  at least one boundedly aborted. A stable nullable-current `(Entity, Property key)`
  slot accepted the same pair twice, preserved exact-key isolation and is the
  strongest explicit coordinator only within the scratch matrix. `SERIALIZABLE`
  produced one commit and one `40001` in its single case but remains incomplete for
  absence, retries and overload. No production schema or contract changed; the next
  choice is whether exact hot-Entity parallelism earns the slot's persistent concept
  over conservative Entity coordination.
- corrected grill method / understandable and technical layers: the User did not
  answer multiplayer Q6 because its first formulation required too much database
  vocabulary. Every future material option must now state, side by side, the plain
  player/World effect and the exact technical state, ownership, transaction,
  contention and cost effect. Each option must explicitly survive the million-User
  and deliberately hot-subject Terry gate; unexplained implementation terms cannot
  be delegated to the User as a decision. `AGENTS.md`, build-aicadia and the generic
  grilling procedure now encode the compact reusable rule. Q6 remains open and will
  be asked again in the corrected form; no Property-slot choice was accepted.
- decided scope / collective settlement only for explicit communal authority: the
  User selected option A after suggesting that high simultaneous
  demand for one Property could open a short Agent proposal, discussion or voting
  window, settle one outcome and briefly protect it from another change. Adversarial
  game and systems reviews support this only as an explicit governance mechanic for
  deliberately communal meaning, never as the correctness layer or a universal
  response to traffic. Exact Property coordination would still serialize settlement;
  World—not user-controlled Agents—would own eligibility, bounds, deadlines,
  deterministic result, atomic Activity and cooldown. Listener counts cannot prove
  intent, subscriptions cannot invoke Agents or spend tokens, and fully open
  million-User voting is not bounded without a separate aggregation, identity and
  abuse design. Physical causality remains direct. Only a concrete operation may
  designate a settlement name, monument, shared creation or comparable World fact
  as communal; traffic, listeners and contention may never create that authority.
  Eligibility, the bounded decision rule and any voting exception to
  `No Score Anywhere` remain open; the subsequent Q6 choice supplies exact Property
  coordination underneath any later collective settlement.
- decided direction / multiplayer Q6 exact Property coordination: the User selected
  option B after its player, technical and million-User behavior was made concrete.
  Independent facts such as one Entity's color and inscription may progress in
  parallel; two mutations of the same exact fact use one canonical lane. This earns
  a stable internal `(Entity, Property key)` coordinator including expected absence,
  while one extremely hot fact must still return bounded accepted, conflict or busy
  outcomes rather than queueing every request. The choice is a product/concurrency
  direction, not a production schema or throughput claim. The User immediately
  opened the next boundary with Tree chopping versus painting, Entity listeners and
  post-change cooldown; cross-Property dependencies and the cooldown's game authority
  remain under grill.
- corrected ownership / World cannot infer semantic destruction: the User rejected
  the implicit assumption that World knows chopping is destructive while painting is
  not. Aicadia has no Entity taxonomy or Property semantics; Agent and User own the
  fictional meaning and exact proposed consequence, while World owns only bounded
  structural validation, submitted exact versions/dependencies, authority, locality,
  atomic state and history. A future typed movement or retirement operation can be
  structurally known, but an Agent-supplied `destructive` label cannot safely grant
  or require mechanics. Entity listeners may announce proposed or accepted change
  but cannot resolve semantics. The next open decision is therefore which ordinary
  actions may voluntarily open a reactable window and which explicitly protected or
  structural authorities may require one.
- decided build rule / intelligence and call assembly belong to Agents: the User
  elevated the prior correction into a permanent methodology rule. World never
  infers kinds, physics, destructiveness, causality, spatial effect or desired
  outcome from Agent-authored content. Explicitly invoked User-owned Agents inspect
  actual World facts and compose bounded typed calls, including intended state,
  claimed dependencies and an explicit multi-Place scope such as an explosion
  affecting A and B. World validates only structural facts it owns, rejects
  unverifiable or unbounded proposals and remains authority over atomic state and
  Activity. When semantic judgment should be collective, bounded eligible Agents may
  propose, deliberate or choose through an Agent-facing protocol while World owns
  deterministic admission and settlement. `AGENTS.md`, build-aicadia and the World/
  Agent glossary now govern this split.
- corrected modeling / no ceremonial lifecycle Properties: exact Property slots do
  not imply that every Tree has `form`, `exists`, `shape`, `state` or any other
  common key. An Agent names only actual or expected-absent facts used by its call;
  World must not manufacture an ontology to make dependency logic convenient. The
  broader candidate is now a property-agnostic Agent intent/deliberation surface,
  including possible nearby-Agent consensus and Agent-authored multi-Place effects.
  Its MCP subscription lifecycle, eligibility, bounded discussion, settlement,
  history, privacy and hotspot behavior are under primary-source research and remain
  unaccepted product design; traffic alone still cannot create authority.
- researched / collective intent assembly and current MCP boundary: three primary-
  source reports now support Agents as semantic package authors and critics, not
  authoritative state processors. MCP `2026-07-28` offers opt-in resource invalidation
  for already-alive hosts followed by authoritative refetch, with no replay, Agent
  invocation or governance; current Aicadia exposes no such resources. LLM and
  social-choice evidence shows bounded diverse drafts can help but agreement can be
  wrong, homogeneous errors correlate, long debate drifts, hostile messages persuade
  or inject, all-to-all context is quadratic and voting needs authoritative identity.
  The strongest unaccepted kernel is sealed independent complete packages, one
  bounded critique/replacement round, an immutable final package, a fixed mechanical
  selection rule and exact World revalidation/atomic settlement. A claimed scope such
  as explosion Places A/B must be structurally witnessed, capability-authorized,
  collectively ratified or rejected; inclusion does not prove that Place C was
  correctly omitted. `Collective intent assembly` is the working phrase because
  conversational agreement, social choice, distributed consensus and World
  settlement are distinct. Activation, eligibility, participant bounds, selection,
  history, stale outcomes, `No Score Anywhere` and cooldown remain unchosen.
- corrected frontier / World cannot choose the deliberation route without a prior
  authority basis: the User rejected the Q7 framing in which World mechanically
  selects advisory or binding assembly, because a semantically dumb World has no
  basis for that choice. The core problem is canonical authorship under semantic
  uncertainty: an intelligent Agent can author meaning, but neither one Agent nor
  conversational agreement proves that its interpretation may become shared truth.
  Listener, deliberation, exact-slot settlement and cooldown solve notification,
  package quality, concurrency and stability respectively; none grants legitimacy.
  The strongest open candidate gives every mutation one uniform shape—exact
  Agent-authored consequence, exact current dependencies, a mechanically verifiable
  settlement basis and atomic World settlement. A basis might be current Character
  control, a delegated capability or collective ratification by Characters with
  structurally established standing. Direct and collective then become one- versus
  multi-authority instances of the same protocol, not semantic modes chosen by
  World. What grants standing over ordinary shared Entities, Places and cross-Place
  consequences is now the prerequisite decision; `standing` remains a working term,
  not canonical vocabulary.
- corrected root problem / massively concurrent Agent-authored World change: the
  User rejected the subsequent promotion of canonical authorship and standing to the
  core problem. Aicadia's actual root is one persistent multiplayer World in which
  millions of Users, through intelligent Agents, concurrently discover, create,
  enrich and manipulate millions of Entities and linked Entities such as Places and
  areas, so World state can change at large scale at any moment. Agents own semantic
  interpretation and consequence assembly; World owns dumb deterministic bounded
  state transition. The design must handle both distributed work and deliberate hot
  subjects without fixed Entity schemas or ceremonial Properties: chopping and
  painting may conflict, differ in consequence or validly compose into a felled blue
  Tree according to Agent reasoning. Authority, listeners, deliberation, exact slots,
  settlement, cooldown and overload return to downstream hypotheses. The session now
  decomposes this root problem step by step and uses bounded experiments rather than
  prematurely selecting one mechanism as the answer.
- decided first decomposition / action-local interest plus Agent-declared effect
  scope: while an Agent prepares a multiplayer Action, every relevant current World
  subject belongs to a bounded active interest set; the Tree and current Place are
  the concrete first case. Resource subscriptions give only stale/refetch hints,
  while the request carries exact expected facts or versions that World always
  revalidates, so lost, duplicate or delayed hints cannot affect correctness and no
  notification invokes an Agent. For a bomb or comparable multi-Place consequence,
  the initiating Agent—not World—declares every affected Place and exact consequence;
  World validates structural scope, bounds, authority and freshness, then invalidates
  each accepted affected resource for active subscribers. Whether resource interest
  and exact commit dependencies are one or two sets is the next open decision.
- researched / portable resource interest before subscription transport: after the
  User stepped back from the two-set decision, three focused primary-source studies
  compared Claude Code, ChatGPT desktop, ChatGPT web and future Agent hosts; MCP,
  raw SSE, WebSocket, webhooks and vendor channels; PostgreSQL propagation; and
  global, Place, exact Entity, private Character and hybrid board scopes. The widest
  defensible cross-host floor is one explicit User turn followed by a bounded
  authoritative MCP read. Core MCP `subscriptions/listen` is a promising optional
  stale-hint accelerator where a host proves it, but current documentation does not
  prove uniform host surfacing or model reaction and no notification may invoke an
  LLM. The strongest unaccepted KISS candidate is one semantic `read` plus optional
  exact-resource `listen`: bounded pull-only World board for shared discovery,
  current-Place discovery and Agent-selected exact Entity focus. A globally
  invalidated live board, durable per-recipient subscription state and one hot World
  revision/cursor are rejected directions. Internally, compact best-effort
  post-commit PostgreSQL dirty hints plus bounded gateway listeners and refetch are
  the smallest candidate; outbox, CDC or broker must be earned by measurement. No
  production behavior changed. The next grill asks whether full play without live
  push is a required portable guarantee before any lab chooses a board or transport.
- corrected product direction / live interest is a core multiplayer mechanic: the
  User rejected Root's recommendation that full play must work without live push.
  Aicadia's value depends on active Characters being kept promptly aware of relevant
  concurrent Entity and Place changes; this must be lightweight, performant and
  designed for millions rather than treated as optional polish. Bounded
  authoritative reads remain mandatory for initial truth, refetch and reconnect,
  but turn-time polling alone is not equivalent full active play. Every supported
  active host must eventually prove a native MCP listen path or a semantically
  equivalent live adapter without server-triggered LLM calls. This corrects the
  immediate concept and T4 plan direction but selects no transport, broker, board
  scope or delivery guarantee yet. The next prerequisite choice is whether every
  relevant accepted Activity must be recoverably delivered or live notifications may
  coalesce while current state and bounded recent context remain recoverable.
- decided direction / multiplayer Q7 live delivery coalesces per resource: the User
  selected option B. During normal operation an active host should receive every
  relevant resource change promptly, but repeated changes to the same resource may
  collapse into one pending stale signal when that host or the system is under
  pressure. Refetch returns authoritative current state and a bounded recent Activity
  window; World retains every accepted Activity once but does not create recipient
  copies, unbounded delivery backlogs or a guarantee that every intermediate change
  enters every Agent context. This preserves required live play while bounding
  memory, queues, reconnect recovery and token exposure for million-User and hot-
  Entity scenarios. It chooses no concrete transport, latency target, queue size,
  Activity window or slow-consumer policy. The next grill chooses the first live
  interest scope before a delivery lab can compare implementations honestly.
- corrected system target / one unified World change substrate: the User rejected
  continuing from experiments that treated transaction coordination, Entity change,
  listeners, scoped effects and collective settlement as several systems. Every
  creation, Property/Trait or later concrete Entity mutation must flow through one
  core change substrate; current state, one Activity, resource dirtiness, live
  refetch and any optional Agent assembly must refer to the same accepted change.
  The system may contain multiple components and introduce new Agent tools when
  needed, but no component may become a parallel mutation path or truth authority.
  Primary-source synthesis now supports one bounded Agent-authored package with
  exact expectations and typed mutations, one deterministic World settlement and
  mechanically derived dirty resources. Huge contextual World change is a candidate
  single scoped effect; literal rewrite of millions of independent Entity facts is
  inherently proportional work and may not masquerade as one instant mutation. The
  earlier listener-scope question is superseded. The multiplayer lab plan returned
  to `draft` for re-acceptance around a unified semantic-kernel, PostgreSQL and live
  delivery experiment sequence; no current game contract or production behavior
  changed. See the [research](../../research/unified-world-change-system.md).
- corrected effect scope and sensory ownership / compact scope plus receiving-Agent
  interpretation: the User rejected requiring an initiating Agent to enumerate every
  Place in a large effect such as one thousand houses inside an audible area. One
  package must be able to reference one bounded structurally verifiable Place/Area or
  topology scope, store its occurrence once and dirty the shared scope resource;
  World owns membership, authorization, bounds and freshness but not the semantic
  reason. Compact occurrence scope does not make actual mutation of one thousand
  independent houses free. Inside an already authorized structural scope, the
  originating Agent may state a sensory condition such as “only Characters that can
  hear,” and each receiving Agent compares that meaning with its own Character's
  authoritative Property/Trait context, silently ignoring the occurrence when it
  was not noticed. World does not interpret Trait prose or Property meaning as a
  hearing rule. Private/targeted authorization and outside-scope filtering remain
  World-owned. Exact scope grammar, membership versioning and historical sensory
  state remain open; see the active [multiplayer concept](../concurrency-and-world-dynamics.md).
- corrected spatial abstraction / no mandatory district, Area or containment layer:
  the User rejected solving large dynamic effects by inventing a `district` or other
  containing map object that the current game does not use. A compact effect must
  instead be grounded in the actual dynamic World: the open candidate is a bounded
  World-evaluated reach from one Entity or Place over explicit current structural
  relations, without making an Agent enumerate one thousand matched houses. This
  correction exposes the deeper missing game model—current Entity state has exact
  placement but no general structural relation, bounded reach or Entity-caused
  effect surface. `Reach`, `Relation` and `Effect` remain working terms pending one
  concrete reactive-world decision; no current game contract or canonical
  vocabulary changed.
- rejected generic executable Relation/Effect / causality needs a mechanical
  contract: the User identified that an arbitrary Agent-authored Relation cannot tell
  World how to propagate deterministically, while an Effect requiring every target
  and outcome up front wrongly makes the initiating Agent own all receiving-side
  meaning. Free Properties, Traits, prose and future descriptive relations therefore
  cannot trigger World behavior. The open replacement separates authoritative direct
  mutation from an occurrence that receiving Agents may interpret, ignore or answer
  through a later explicit causal Action. Only a concrete World-owned mechanical
  capability, causal link or structural selector with fixed authority, bounds,
  cycle and conflict behavior could propagate automatically; a trigger may dirty a
  resource or admit a reaction opportunity but may never invoke an Agent. Candidate
  terms remain unaccepted and `game/docs/`/`dev/CONTEXT.md` are unchanged.
- rejected mechanism-first causal architecture / reset to a flat game model: the
  User rejected the proposed Affordance, causal-link and mechanical-runtime direction
  as too broad, abstract and insufficiently collaborative. Current exploration
  returns to World game-state, Entity, Relation, Action, Interaction and Consequence;
  none receives a new implementation or canonical definition yet. One smallest
  concrete scene must first resolve who acts, what state exists before, what the
  Agent submits, what changes immediately, what another Agent can influence and what
  state and history remain. The draft lab plan stays non-executable and
  `game/docs/`, `dev/CONTEXT.md` and code remain unchanged.

# 2026-08-17

## Aicadia Studio Rust prototype accepted

- **Status:** accepted; implementation active.
- **Choice:** accept `dev/plans/20260817-140952-aicadia-studio-prototype/plan.md` and rebuild the existing Rust local application at `/` into the first Aicadia Studio comparison prototype. `cargo dev` remains the sole developer entrypoint; `Game` and `Live` consume one Rust-produced, read-only projection; the three interface hierarchies remain alternatives until observed use selects one.
- **Reason:** the corrected plan preserves the User's Rust-only application boundary, absorbs the existing ledger instead of creating a second app, and tests the documentation/World overview against real repository and local-World sources without duplicating authored truth.
- **Affected scope:** local development interface, Rust Studio projection, existing browser route, local launcher wording and supporting development documentation/evidence. No game schema, World behavior, MCP capability or Agent contract changes.

## Aicadia Studio — prototype direction selected

- user direction / accepted development-interface shape: name the development
  application **Aicadia Studio**, use English, and organize it around `Game` and
  `Live`. `Game` projects the accepted game contract, models, MCP tools, rules,
  questions, experiments, evidence and history from their owning sources; `Live`
  inspects one selected World read-only. Mechanically available fields, relations
  and tool schemas should be generated or introspected, while semantic meaning is
  authored once in its owning Markdown source. Small project-structure changes are
  allowed only when they materially improve total clarity.
- selected prototype-first method: build a quick, visibly throwaway, source-backed
  UI comparison before selecting production structure. The prototype may use a
  parser only to project existing authorities and may later justify a static
  generator when one concrete seam cannot be represented otherwise; generated
  output never becomes a second authority. The current game remains one World with
  no durable `WorldId`, so a future World selector is connection-scoped UI only and
  chooses no multi-World storage model. Current game behavior, schema, Agent tools
  and the shipped ledger remain unchanged. See the active
  [Studio concept](../aicadia-studio.md) and draft
  [prototype plan](../../../../dev/plans/20260817-140952-aicadia-studio-prototype/plan.md).
- corrected implementation architecture / existing Rust application becomes Studio:
  the User rejected the separate Python/lab application completely. Aicadia
  application logic, source parsing, catalog assembly, database access and server
  behavior must always be Rust. The existing `cargo dev` application and `/` World
  ledger are rebuilt in place into Aicadia Studio; `Live` absorbs the ledger while
  `Game` adds source-backed development visibility. Existing HTML/CSS/JavaScript may
  remain presentation-only and owns no game or documentation truth. This correction
  supersedes every Python, separate-process and `lab/studio` assumption in the prior
  draft without changing current game behavior, schema or tools. The Studio plan is
  revised in place and remains `draft` pending explicit acceptance.
